import QtQuick 2.0
MenuContainer {
    id: fermentable
    anchors.fill: parent
    anchors.bottomMargin: em(1)
    radius: em(1)
    height: (isEditing) ? em(14) : em(3)
    primaryHeader: (isEditing) ? ((index + 1) + ". MALT") : ""
    property int index
    property int bagIndex
    property string customName
    property string name
    property real amount
    property int minimum: 0
    property int maximum: 8000
    property int absmaximum: 8000
    hasRemove: true
    onRemove: views.fermentablesView.removeFermentable(bagIndex, index);
    function load() {
        fermentableTypeLoader.active = true;
    }
    Component {
        id: fermentableType
        Selector{
            anchors.fill: parent
            selectorType: "fermentable"
            key: "name"
            value: name
            onItemChanged: views.fermentablesView.setType(bagIndex, fermentable.index, item);
        }
    }
    Component {
        id: fermentableAmount
        EditWeightSmall {
            editedHeader: layout.ingredients.primaryHeader
            label: (isEditing) ? "Amount" : ""
            baseNumber: (amount!==null) ? amount : 0
            absoluteMin: 0
            absoluteMax: (recipeHandler.activeRecipe!==null) ? Math.floor(recipeHandler.activeRecipe.bounds.leftAmount*1000) : 8000//maximum
            onSave: views.fermentablesView.setAmount(bagIndex, index, baseNumber);
            onEdited: views.fermentablesView.setAmount(bagIndex, index, baseNumber);
            onClicked: {
                var data = views.fermentablesView.data;
                var length = data.length;
                if(bagIndex>=0 && bagIndex<length) {
                    data = data[bagIndex];
                    length = data.length;
                    var amount = 0;
                    var item;
                    for(var i=0; i<length; i++) {
                        item = data[i];
                        if(i!==index && item.hasOwnProperty("amount")) {
                            amount += item.amount;
                        }
                    }
                    recipeHandler.activeRecipe.changeBoundsAmount(amount);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Loader {
        id: fermentableTypeLoader
        anchors.top: parent.topAnchor
        anchors.topMargin: (isEditing) ? -em(1) : em(0.5)
        anchors.right: parent.right
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.leftMargin: em(1)
        anchors.rightMargin: (isEditing) ? em(1) : em(10)
        height: (isEditing) ? em(6) : em(3)
        active: false
        sourceComponent: fermentableType
        onLoaded: fermentableAmountLoader.active = true;
    }
    Loader {
        id: fermentableAmountLoader
        anchors.top: (isEditing) ? fermentableTypeLoader.bottom : fermentableTypeLoader.top
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        active: false
        sourceComponent: fermentableAmount
    }

Rectangle {
    id: fullButton
    height: em(5)
    color: buttonColor
    radius: px(10)
    signal clicked
    property string label : label
    property string buttonColor : "#f47b32"
    Text {
        anchors.centerIn: parent
        color: "#ffffff"
        font.family: montserratExtraBold
        font.pixelSize: px(28)
        font.bold: true
        text: label
    }
    MouseArea {
        z: 1
        anchors.fill: parent
        onPressed: fullButton.color = Qt.darker(fullButton.color, 1.25);
        onReleased: {
            fullButton.clicked();
            fullButton.color = Qt.lighter(fullButton.color, 1.25);
        }
    }
import QtQuick 2.0
MenuContainer {
    id: hoppingItem
    anchors.fill: parent
    anchors.topMargin: em(0.5)
    height: parent.height
    color: (isEditing) ? "#282828" : "transparent"
    hasUnderline: true
    property int index
    property var hops
    function load() {
        timeLoader.active = true;
    }
    primaryHeader: "HOP CAGE "
    circles: index
    Component {
        id: list
        ListView {
            spacing: 0
            interactive: false
            model: hops
            delegate: Item{
                height: em(3)
                EditWeightSmall {
                    anchors.left: parent.left
                    anchors.right: parent.right
                    label: (modelData.name!==undefined) ? modelData.name : "No selected hops"
                    baseNumber: (modelData.amount!==undefined) ? modelData.amount : 0
                    alwaysInactive: true
                    hasStep: false
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Component {
        id: time
        EditNumber {
            anchors.left: parent.left
            anchors.right: parent.right
            label: "Time"
            unit: "min"
            min: 0
            max: 180
            absoluteMin: 0
            absoluteMax: 180
            baseNumber: (hops.length>0 && hops[0].time!==undefined && !isNaN(hops[0].time)) ? hops[0].time.toString() : 0
            number: (hops.length>0 && hops[0].time!==undefined && !isNaN(hops[0].time)) ? hops[0].time.toString() : 0
            editedHeader: hoppingItem.primaryHeader
            onEdited: views.hoppingView.setCageTime(index, parseInt(number));
        }
    }
    Loader {
        id: listLoader
        anchors.top: parent.topAnchor
        anchors.topMargin: em(0.5)
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        height: em(3)*hops.length
        asynchronous: true
        active: false
        sourceComponent: list
    }
    Loader {
        id: timeLoader
        anchors.top: listLoader.bottom
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        active: false
        sourceComponent: time
        onLoaded: listLoader.active = true;
    }
import QtQuick 2.0
Rectangle {
    id:cooling
    anchors.fill: parent
    anchors.leftMargin: parent.width
    color: "#141414"
    visible: false
    property real dataValue : views.coolingView.data
    onDataValueChanged: temperature.baseNumber = (dataValue>=0) ? dataValue : Number.NaN;
    function show() {
        if(!showAnimation.running) {
            showAnimation.running = true;
            visible = true;
        }
    }
    function hide() {
        if(!hideAnimation.running) {
            hideAnimation.running = true;
        }
    }
    function reset() {
        if(visible) {
            anchors.leftMargin = parent.width;
            visible = false;
        }
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: showAnimation
        running: false
        to: 0
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.OutQuad
        onStopped: information.visible = true
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: hideAnimation
        running: false
        to: parent.width
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.InQuad
        onStarted: information.visible = false
        onStopped: visible = false;
    }
    Text {
        id: target
        anchors.top: parent.top
        anchors.topMargin: em(3)
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.leftMargin: em(2)
        text: "Target temperature"
        font.capitalization: Font.AllUppercase
        color: "#ffffff"
        font.pixelSize: px(24)
        font.bold: true
        font.family: montserratExtraBold
    }
    EditTemperature {
        id: temperature
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        anchors.leftMargin: em(1)
        anchors.rightMargin: em(1)
        anchors.top: target.bottom
        anchors.topMargin: em(2)
        editedHeader: target.text
        min: 0
        max: 100
        absoluteMin: 0
        absoluteMax: 100
        label: "Temperature"
        step: 0.1
        onEdited: views.coolingView.setTemperature(baseNumber);
    }
    Text {
        id: information
        anchors.top: temperature.bottom
        anchors.topMargin: em(2)
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.leftMargin: em(2)
        anchors.rightMargin: em(2)
        visible: false
        anchors.right: parent.right
        text: "Cooling temperature depends on your tap water's temperature.\n\nMinimum cooling temperature:\ntap water temperature +5
C\n\nIf no cooling value has been set, the machine will cool to the lowest degree depending on the inlet water temperature."
        wrapMode: Text.Wrap
        color: "#e67526"
        font.pixelSize: px(24)
        font.italic: true
        font.family: latoRegular
    }

Rectangle {
    id: connectionChooser
    anchors.fill: parent
    anchors.topMargin: em(8)
    anchors.leftMargin: parent.width
    color: "#141414"
    visible: false
    property var dataList : connectionHandler.networks
    function show() {
        layout.mainMenu.activeScreen = layout.connections;
        layout.header.label = "Choose connection";
        layout.header.hasBack = true;
        layout.header.hasTag = true;
        if(!showAnimation.running) {
            showAnimation.running = true;
            visible = true;
        }
    }
    function hide() {
        layout.settings.show();
        if(!hideAnimation.running) {
            hideAnimation.running = true;
        }
        layout.header.hasTag = false
    }
    function reset() {
        anchors.leftMargin = parent.width;
        visible = false;
        layout.header.hasTag = false
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: showAnimation
        running: false
        to: 0
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.OutQuad
        onStopped: layout.settings.reset();
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: hideAnimation
        running: false
        to: parent.width
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.InQuad
        onStopped: visible = false;
    }
    ListView {
        id: list
        anchors.top: parent.top
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        anchors.bottom: parent.bottom
        clip: true
        spacing: 4
        model: dataList
        delegate: ConnectionListItem {
            anchors.left: parent.left
            anchors.right: parent.right
            name: modelData.essid
            strength: (modelData.quality!==undefined) ? modelData.quality : 0
            security: (modelData.type!==undefined) ? modelData.type : 0
            status: (modelData.state!==undefined) ? modelData.state : 0
            MouseArea {
                anchors.fill: parent
                z: 9
                onClicked: layout.connectionSelector.setData(modelData.index);
            }
        }
    }

2import QtQuick 2.0
Rectangle{
    id: calibration
    anchors.fill: parent
    anchors.topMargin: em(8)
    anchors.leftMargin: parent.width
    color: "#141414"
    visible: false
    function show() {
        layout.mainMenu.activeScreen = layout.calibration;
        layout.header.label = "Calibration";
        layout.header.hasBack = true;
        configurationHandler.initCalibration();
        if(!showAnimation.running) {
            showAnimation.running = true;
            visible = true;
        }
    }
    function hide() {
        calibrationValue.baseNumber = -1;
        layout.settings.show();
        if(!hideAnimation.running) {
            hideAnimation.running = true;
        }
    }
    function reset() {
        calibrationValue.baseNumber = -1;
        anchors.leftMargin = parent.width;
        visible = false;
    }
    function setFocus() {
        calibrationValue.setFocus();
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: showAnimation
        running: false
        to: 0
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.OutQuad
        onStopped: layout.settings.reset();
    }
    NumberAnimation on anchors.leftMargin {
        id: hideAnimation
        running: false
        to: parent.width
        duration: 500
        easing.type: Easing.InQuad
        onStopped: visible = false;
    }
    EditWeightSmall {
        id: calibrationValue
        anchors.top: parent.top
        anchors.topMargin: em(1)
        anchors.left: parent.left
        anchors.right: parent.right
        alwaysActive: true
        hasStep: false
        editedHeader: "Calibration water amount"
        absoluteMin: 7000
        absoluteMax: 10000
        exception: 43373
        onBaseNumberChanged: {
            if(baseNumber>0) {
                if(baseNumber===43.373) {
                    debug.visible = true;
                    baseNumber = Number.NaN;
                    reset();
                } else {
                    configurationHandler.setCalibration(baseNumber*1000);
                }
            }
        }
    }
